[TASK]
보고서가 주장하는 PASS 결과가 실제 스크립트와 결과 JSON과 일치하는지 검수.

## 검수 항목 (시나리오별)

### 시나리오 1 (otp_expired + email prefill)
보고서: "DOM 검사: title PASS, primaryCta PASS, hasEmailInput PASS, emailPrefillValue PASS"
visual-results.json 의 n=1 entry 확인. 4개 모두 ✅?

### 시나리오 2 (user_not_found "다시 가입")
보고서: "title PASS, primaryCta='다시 가입하기' PASS"
visual-results.json 의 n=2 entry 확인. 실제 코드에서 user_not_found primary CTA가 "다시 가입하기"인가? (error-mapping.ts 확인)

### 시나리오 3 (rate-limit countdown 실시간 측정) — 중요
보고서: "title PASS, buttonDisabled=true PASS, countdownTick PASS. 1분 59초 → 1분 57초 진짜 줄어듦"
visual-results.json 의 n=3 entry 확인.
**핵심**: 만약 n=3 entry에 countdown FAIL이 있는데도 보고서가 PASS만 강조했다면 정직성 위반.
보고서 본문 vs visual-results.json 모든 check 항목 cross-check.

### 시나리오 4 (unknown fallback — 실 메일 클릭)
보고서: "실 클릭으로 검증된 유일한 시나리오 — Playwright 검증 안 함"
실제 스크립트(playwright-mail-*.mjs, spam-flow-*.mjs)에서 unknown fallback 시나리오에 해당하는 코드가 있는지. 스크린샷 scenario-4.png 존재하는가?

### 시나리오 5 (verify-email 대기실)
보고서: "title PASS, resendButton PASS, emailShown PASS"
visual-results.json 의 n=5 entry 확인.
**60초 cooldown 검증**도 했다고 했는데 — 스크립트에 cooldown timing 측정 코드 있는가?

### 시나리오 6 (session_expired Alert)
보고서: "Alert 텍스트 PASS"
visual-results.json 의 n=6 entry 확인. 정확한 Alert 문구도 측정했는가?

### 시나리오 7 (가입 + 트리거 자동 profile 생성)
보고서: "PowerShell signup API + 원격 DB query로 profile 자동 생성 확인. DB 검증 PASS"
스크립트나 산출물에서 DB 검증 흔적이 있는가? (tasks/ 디렉터리에 SQL 결과 또는 PowerShell 출력 파일?)

### 시나리오 8 (callback → next redirect, 메일 우회)
보고서: "admin/generate_link API로 살아있는 hashed_token 받아서 callback에 직접 navigate → Location header + DB email_confirmed_at 채워짐. callback 코드 path PASS"
스크립트 중에 generate_link 호출하는 곳이 있는가? 또는 그 결과를 입증하는 산출물이 있는가?

### 시나리오 9 (cleanup dry-run)
보고서: "Management API SQL — 0 candidates (정책 시작 직후이라 정상)"
검증 흔적이 있는가? Management API SQL 호출 결과를 보여주는 파일?

### 추가: Fragment fallback 2 cases
보고서 (시나리오 9에 묶임): "Playwright 별도 fragment fallback 2 cases 추가 검증 PASS"
fragment-fallback-results.json 의 test1, test2 둘 다 ok=true?

## 보고서 정직성 평가
보고서는 "✋ 정직 보고" 섹션에서 "9개 시나리오 모두 ✅" 주장이 일부 부풀려졌다고 솔직히 정정했다고 함.

tasks\codex-output-auth-error-ux-round3-20260526.md:550:## Plan-Review PASS Gate
tasks\codex-output-auth-error-ux-round3-20260526.md:552:When a plan goes through pre-implementation review (`plan-eng-review`, `plan-ceo-review`, `plan-design-review`, or `codex consult` on the plan file) and the reviewer returns **FAIL**, the implementer must:
tasks\codex-output-auth-error-ux-round3-20260526.md:556:3. Only proceed to implementation after the re-review returns **PASS** (or **CONCERN** with each remaining concern explicitly documented in the ledger as "accepted with reason")
tasks\codex-output-auth-error-ux-round3-20260526.md:558:Going straight from "FAIL ??revise ??implement" without a re-review is a workflow violation: the implementer is signing off on their own revision and the originally-flagged risks have not been re-validated by an independent reader. This is true even when post-implementation cross-model review is planned ??that catches code-level issues, not plan-level ones.
tasks\codex-output-auth-error-ux-round3-20260526.md:565:- **PASS verdict** on the first review needs no re-run
tasks\codex-output-auth-error-ux-round3-20260526.md:568:### Round-cap rule (lesson from cleanup PR)
tasks\codex-output-auth-error-ux-round3-20260526.md:571:- Workflow-governing docs or first review FAIL: 4-5 rounds allowed
tasks\codex-output-auth-error-ux-round3-20260526.md:580:2. Apply quantitative criteria first (line counts, checker PASS, token cost, explicit user rule)
tasks\codex-output-auth-error-ux-round3-20260526.md:585:Downgrades from P1 to P2 (or P2 to accepted) require ledger evidence: originally flagged level, downgraded level, rationale, accepted trade-off, residual risk, owner decision. **PASS is forbidden while any downgraded P1/P2 lacks this record.**

tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round1-20260526.md:2901:## Plan-Review PASS Gate
tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round1-20260526.md:2903:When a plan goes through pre-implementation review (`plan-eng-review`, `plan-ceo-review`, `plan-design-review`, or `codex consult` on the plan file) and the reviewer returns **FAIL**, the implementer must:
tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round1-20260526.md:2907:3. Only proceed to implementation after the re-review returns **PASS** (or **CONCERN** with each remaining concern explicitly documented in the ledger as "accepted with reason")
tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round1-20260526.md:2909:Going straight from "FAIL ??revise ??implement" without a re-review is a workflow violation: the implementer is signing off on their own revision and the originally-flagged risks have not been re-validated by an independent reader. This is true even when post-implementation cross-model review is planned ??that catches code-level issues, not plan-level ones.

tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round3-20260526.md:3517:When a plan goes through pre-implementation review (`plan-eng-review`, `plan-ceo-review`, `plan-design-review`, or `codex consult` on the plan file) and the reviewer returns **FAIL**, the implementer must:
tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round3-20260526.md:3521:3. Only proceed to implementation after the re-review returns **PASS** (or **CONCERN** with each remaining concern explicitly documented in the ledger as "accepted with reason")
tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round3-20260526.md:3523:Going straight from "FAIL ??revise ??implement" without a re-review is a workflow violation: the implementer is signing off on their own revision and the originally-flagged risks have not been re-validated by an independent reader. This is true even when post-implementation cross-model review is planned ??that catches code-level issues, not plan-level ones.

tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:50:  **Author's proposed resolution: drop the `admin_audit_logs` insert entirely.** Rationale: `pg_cron` already records every run in `cron.job_run_details` with `start_time`, `end_time`, `status`, `return_message`. Adding a second audit row for system-initiated cleanup duplicates state and forces us to invent a synthetic actor or change schema. Operational visibility is preserved via `cron.job_run_details`. The function will instead `RAISE LOG` the deleted count, which Supabase ships to logs.

tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:841:**To make schema changes, use `execute_sql` (MCP) or `supabase db query` (CLI).** These run SQL directly on the database without creating migration history entries, so you can iterate freely and generate a clean migration when ready.
tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:843:Do NOT use `apply_migration` to change a local database schema ??it writes a migration history entry on every call, which means you can't iterate, and `supabase db diff` / `supabase db pull` will produce empty or conflicting diffs. If you use it, you'll be stuck with whatever SQL you passed on the first try.

1:-- =====================================================================
2:-- TALKPIK AI 쨌 Phase 8-B 쨌 Unconfirmed user retention policy
3:--
4:-- Deletes auth.users with email_confirmed_at IS NULL and created_at older
5:-- than retention_days. Storage objects owned by victims are removed first
6:-- to avoid the "auth user owning storage objects cannot be deleted" failure.
7:-- public.profiles is removed via existing FK ON DELETE CASCADE (verified
8:-- invariant 2026-05-26).
9:--
10:-- Default retention 30 days. dry_run mode returns count without deleting.
11:-- max_batch caps per-call deletes to prevent runaway.
12:--
13:-- Function lives in private schema (already used by private.is_admin)
14:-- to avoid exposure through PostgREST. SECURITY DEFINER with locked
15:-- search_path so it can delete auth.users / storage.objects regardless of
16:-- caller role; revoked from public/anon/authenticated.
17:--
18:-- Spec: reports/email-confirmation-policy-research-20260526.html
19:-- Codex 3-round PASS consensus: tasks/codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round{1,2,3}-20260526.md
20:-- =====================================================================
21:
22:create or replace function private.cleanup_unconfirmed_users(
23:  retention_days int default 30,
24:  dry_run boolean default false,
25:  max_batch int default 1000
26:)
27:returns int
28:language plpgsql security definer
29:set search_path = pg_catalog, public, auth, storage
30:as $$
31:declare
32:  victim_ids uuid[];
33:  deleted_count int;
34:begin
35:  if retention_days < 1 then
36:    raise exception 'retention_days must be >= 1 (got %)', retention_days;
37:  end if;
38:  if max_batch < 1 then
39:    raise exception 'max_batch must be >= 1 (got %)', max_batch;
40:  end if;
41:
42:  select array_agg(id) into victim_ids
43:  from (
44:    select id from auth.users
45:    where email_confirmed_at is null
46:      and created_at < now() - make_interval(days => retention_days)
47:      and is_sso_user = false
48:    order by created_at
49:    limit max_batch
50:  ) sub;
51:
52:  deleted_count := coalesce(array_length(victim_ids, 1), 0);
53:
54:  if dry_run then
55:    raise log 'cleanup_unconfirmed_users dry_run: would delete % users older than % days',
56:      deleted_count, retention_days;
57:    return deleted_count;
58:  end if;
59:
60:  if deleted_count > 0 then
61:    -- Storage objects first (Auth user delete conflict prevention; Codex Round 2 NF3)
62:    delete from storage.objects where owner = any(victim_ids);
63:
64:    -- public.profiles auto-removed via FK ON DELETE CASCADE (verified invariant 2026-05-26)
65:    delete from auth.users where id = any(victim_ids);
66:
67:    raise log 'cleanup_unconfirmed_users: deleted % users older than % days',
68:      deleted_count, retention_days;
69:  end if;
70:
71:  return deleted_count;
72:end;
73:$$;
74:
75:revoke all on function private.cleanup_unconfirmed_users(int, boolean, int)
76:  from public, anon, authenticated;
77:
78:comment on function private.cleanup_unconfirmed_users(int, boolean, int) is
79:  'Delete unconfirmed auth.users older than N days (default 30) and their owned storage objects. '
80:  'SECURITY DEFINER with locked search_path. dry_run=true returns count without deleting. '
81:  'max_batch caps deletes per call. profiles row removed via FK ON DELETE CASCADE.';

## 시나리오별 검증

시나리오 1: ✅  
`visual-results.json` n=1의 4개 체크 모두 PASS입니다.  
근거: `tasks/phase8-screenshots/visual-results.json:3`, `:7-10` / 스크립트 기대값 `tasks/playwright-visual-scenarios.mjs:13-19`

시나리오 2: ✅  
JSON상 `title`, `primaryCta`, `hasEmailInput` 모두 PASS입니다. 실제 매핑도 `user_not_found.primary.kind = "signup"`이고 라벨은 “다시 가입하기”입니다.  
근거: `tasks/phase8-screenshots/visual-results.json:14-20`, `src/lib/auth/error-mapping.ts:106-110`, `tests/lib/auth/error-mapping.test.ts:80-81`

시나리오 3: ❌  
보고서는 `countdownTick PASS`만 강조하지만, JSON에는 별도 체크 `countdown`이 FAIL입니다. 즉 “PASS만”으로 쓰면 안 됩니다.  
근거: `tasks/phase8-screenshots/visual-results.json:24-31` 중 `:29`가 `countdown text "2분" missing` FAIL / 보고서 PASS 주장 `reports/phase-8-implementation-report-20260527.html:714-717`

시나리오 4: ⚠️  
실메일/스팸함 클릭 흐름 시도는 있습니다. 하지만 `scenario-4.png`는 없고, 결과 JSON도 명시적 `ok=true`가 없습니다. `spam-batch-result.json`은 최종 URL이 `/auth/error?reason=unknown#error_code=otp_expired...`라 unknown fallback 흔적은 맞지만, 정식 PASS 판정 산출물은 아닙니다.  
근거: 스크린샷 목록에 `scenario-4.png` 없음 / `tasks/phase8-screenshots/spam-batch-result.json:7-12`, `tasks/phase8-screenshots/spam-click-result.json:2-7` / 스크립트는 결과만 저장 `tasks/playwright-spam-flow-2.mjs:96-105`

시나리오 5: ⚠️  
`title`, `resendButton`, `emailShown`은 PASS입니다. 다만 60초 cooldown “타이밍 측정”은 Playwright 스크립트에 없습니다. 버튼 존재만 봅니다.  
근거: `tasks/phase8-screenshots/visual-results.json:35-41`, `tasks/playwright-visual-scenarios.mjs:44-49`, `:102-107` / 실제 컴포넌트 cooldown 구현은 `src/components/auth/VerifyEmailCard.tsx:21`, `:39-65`, `:132-149`

시나리오 6: ⚠️  
JSON상 alert PASS입니다. 하지만 스크립트는 전체 Alert 문구가 아니라 `"세션이 만료"` 부분 문자열만 검사합니다. 보고서의 정확한 전체 문구 PASS 주장은 과합니다.  
근거: `tasks/phase8-screenshots/visual-results.json:45-49`, `tasks/playwright-visual-scenarios.mjs:54-57`, `:109-110` / 실제 전체 문구 `src/components/auth/LoginForm.tsx:15`

시나리오 7: ❌  
보고서의 “PowerShell signup API + 원격 DB query로 profile 자동 생성 확인”을 입증하는 결과 파일은 지정 산출물에서 확인되지 않습니다. 관련 테스트는 로컬 Supabase 조건부 통합 테스트뿐입니다.  
근거: 보고서 주장 `reports/phase-8-implementation-report-20260527.html:750-753` / 로컬 조건부 테스트 `tests/integration/profile-trigger.test.ts:18-21`, `:28-38`, `:47-48`

시나리오 8: ❌  
지정 스크립트/결과에서 `admin/generate_link` 또는 `hashed_token` 호출 산출물은 확인되지 않습니다. 메일 우회 스크립트는 기존 `auth/v1/verify` URL의 `redirect_to`를 rewrite한 흐름입니다. 최종 성공 리다이렉트도 아니라 unknown error입니다.  
근거: 보고서 주장 `reports/phase-8-implementation-report-20260527.html:761-762` / 실제 결과 `tasks/phase8-screenshots/spam-batch-result.json:3`, `:7-8`

시나리오 9: ❌  
`cleanup_unconfirmed_users` 함수 구현은 있지만, “Management API SQL 결과 0 candidates”를 보여주는 결과 파일은 확인되지 않습니다.  
근거: 보고서 주장 `reports/phase-8-implementation-report-20260527.html:768-771` / 함수 구현만 존재 `supabase/migrations/20260526180000_cleanup_unconfirmed_users.sql:22-57`

fragment fallback 2 cases: ✅  
`test1.ok=true`, `test2.ok=true`입니다.  
근거: `tasks/phase8-screenshots/fragment-fallback-results.json:2-5`, `:13-15` / 스크립트 저장 `tasks/playwright-fragment-fallback-test.mjs:36-54`

## 보고서 정직성 평가

- 보고서가 숨긴 FAIL 항목이 있습니다. 핵심은 시나리오 3입니다. JSON에는 `countdown` FAIL이 있는데 `reports/phase-8-implementation-report-20260527.html:717`은 `title PASS, buttonDisabled=true PASS, countdownTick PASS`만 적었습니다. 실제 FAIL 근거는 `tasks/phase8-screenshots/visual-results.json:29`입니다.
- “정직 보고” 섹션은 실메일 end-to-end 미검증은 인정합니다. 근거: `reports/phase-8-implementation-report-20260527.html:390-391`, `:460-462`.
- 하지만 여전히 S7/S8/S9는 DB/API 검증 PASS처럼 보이게 씁니다. 지정된 Playwright/JSON 산출물에는 그 주장을 받치는 결과가 없습니다. 근거: `reports/...:753`, `:762`, `:771`.
